% Rule 4: member(Value, [_ | Rest]) :- member(Value, Rest).
% Rule 5: literal(Text, Codes, Rest) :- string_codes(Text, Prefix), append(Prefix, Rest, Codes).

% Rule 7: field_tail([32 | Rest], [], Rest) :- .

% Rule 14: hex_code(Code) :- Code >= 97, Code =< 102.

% Rule 18: parsed(Log, Context) :- raw_log(Log, Text), atom_codes(Text, Codes), parse_log(Codes, Context).
% Rule 22: parsed_event(Log, Event, User, Ip, Traceid) :- parsed(Log, Context), member(event(Event), Context), member(user(User), Context), member(ip(Ip), Context), member(trace_id(Traceid), Context).
% Rule 23: trace_alert(User, Traceid, Ip) :- parsed_event(Loginlog, login_failed, User, Ip, Traceid), parsed_event(Paymentlog, payment_denied, User, Ip, Traceid), Loginlog \= Paymentlog.
